## 装备与条目详情

## 40-K Meltagun

**分类：** 战略配备 (`stratagems`) · **ID：** `40-k-meltagun`

**Wiki 原始词条：** https://helldivers.wiki.gg/wiki/40-K_Meltagun

### 简介

The 40-K Meltagun is a Stratagem that calls down a Support Weapon that fires a superheated beam of thermal energy, dealing massive damage and penetrating through large amounts of armor.

|---|---|

## A/AC-8 Autocannon Sentry

**分类：** 战略配备 (`stratagems`) · **ID：** `a-ac-8-autocannon-sentry`

> ⚠️ 原始 Wiki 数据被标记为 `potentially_outdated`，数值请结合最新补丁核对。

**Wiki 原始词条：** https://helldivers.wiki.gg/wiki/A/AC-8_Autocannon_Sentry

### 简介

The A/AC-8 Autocannon Sentry is a powerful defensive stratagem firing armor-piercing, high-explosive shells. It fires in 3 round bursts and has the slowest turning speed of all the automatic sentries. Unlike the AC-8 Autocannon support weapon, the rounds that the sentry fires do not bounce, have higher armor penetration, and will always explode on impact.

## Hive Breaker Drill

**分类：** 战略配备 (`stratagems`) · **ID：** `hive-breaker-drill`

**Wiki 原始词条：** https://helldivers.wiki.gg/wiki/Hive_Breaker_Drill

### 简介

The Hive Breaker Drill Stratagem is only available on the Nuke Nursery mission and is used to start the main objective, and is heavily based on the Tectonic Drill. Once called in, the Hive Breaker Drill is delivered to a fixed location at the drill site via Pelican. It initially drops in a large casing, which will unpack itself after a few seconds to reveal its terminal.

## SEAF Artillery

**分类：** 战略配备 (`stratagems`) · **ID：** `seaf-artillery`

**Wiki 原始词条：** https://helldivers.wiki.gg/wiki/SEAF_Artillery

### 简介

SEAF Artillery is a generic Optional Objective consisting of a large, stationary artillery platform. To complete the objective, Helldivers must activate the artillery, load five shells into it and then connect it to their Super Destroyer. Upon completion, all Helldivers in the mission gain access to five uses of the SEAF Artillery stratagem.

**Wiki 原始词条：** https://helldivers.wiki.gg/wiki/Tactical_Video_Camera

### 简介

The Tactical Video Camera Stratagem is an objective critical stratagem that is a Primary Objective of the Commando: Acquire Evidence mission and is used in one of the potential objectives in Infiltrate Illuminate Lair.

| 输入指令 (`code`) | right, down, down, up, left, left, up |
| `cooldownVariants` | — |
| `shipModules` | — |

| `rotating` | — |

## Tectonic Drill

**分类：** 战略配备 (`stratagems`) · **ID：** `tectonic-drill`

**Wiki 原始词条：** https://helldivers.wiki.gg/wiki/Tectonic_Drill

### 简介

The Tectonic Drill Stratagem allows Helldivers to call down a drill when at a suitable Payload Site. Helldivers must insert a Dark Fluid Vessel inside the drill, and defend it until it can be drilled into the planet. It is event-exclusive, only available in the Deploy Dark Fluid mission.

| `cooldownVariants\[1\].label` | With Morale Augmentation |
| `cooldownVariants\[1\].seconds` | 0 |
| 呼叫时间（秒） (`callInTimeS`) | 10 |
| 使用次数 (`uses`) | unlimited |
| `shipModules` | — |


### 获取方式

| 字段 | 数值 |
|---|---|
| `type` | other |
| `label` |  |
| 战争债券 ID (`warbondId`) | — |

| 消耗 (`cost`) | — |
| `rotating` | — |
